# Appendix 3: E-ITS to NIST Crosswalk (Heuristic)

This appendix provides a complete measure-level heuristic crosswalk from the E-ITS 2024 measure catalogue to NIST CSF 2.0 categories and NIST SP 800-53 Rev. 5 control families. The mapping is based on dominant control intent and is not an official equivalence mapping.

Source population: Appendix 2, generated from the RIA E-ITS 2024 measures table. Accessed: May 1, 2026.

Validation: 1,802 measure rows; 1,802 unique measure IDs; no blank NIST mapping rows; syntactically valid CSF category and SP 800-53 family codes.
